// ============================================================================// KALIYUGA: VAITARANI SIMPLE ASH HUB — WALKABLE 2.5D SANCTUARY LOGIC// Pure deterministic hub geometry & station interaction helpers:// - Savitri (Lotus_Empress 3D) beside her Boat & Lantern (west shoreline)// - Homa Fire Shrine (center-south sanctum brazier)// - Northern Ferry Gate (cross into Act I Chamber I)// ============================================================================export type HubStationId = 'savitri_lantern' | 'homa_shrine' | 'ferry_gate';export interface HubStationSpec {  id: HubStationId;  x: number;  y: number;  interactRadiusPx: number;  title: string;  sanskritLabel: string;  promptHint: string;  accentColor: string;}export const HUB_SPAWN_X = 640;export const HUB_SPAWN_Y = 470;export const HUB_SAVITRI_POS = { x: 310, y: 455 };export const HUB_FERRY_GATE_POS = { x: 640, y: 205 };export const VAITARANI_HUB_STATIONS: HubStationSpec[] = [  {    id: 'savitri_lantern',    x: 345,    y: 475,    interactRadiusPx: 135,    title: 'Savitri’s Boat & Lantern',    sanskritLabel: 'सावित्री नौका · LANTERN CHRONICLE',    promptHint: 'Examine Savitri’s Lantern & Sanjaya Chronicle',    accentColor: '#f2c45a',  },  {    id: 'homa_shrine',    x: 925,    y: 475,    interactRadiusPx: 130,    title: 'Homa Fire Shrine',    sanskritLabel: 'होम वेदी · SHRINE & ARSENAL FORGE',    promptHint: 'Spend Ashen Karma & Temper Weapon Aspects',    accentColor: '#ff7a33',  },  {    id: 'ferry_gate',    x: 640,    y: 215,    interactRadiusPx: 125,    title: 'Charnel Plains Crossing',    sanskritLabel: 'कुरुक्षेत्र द्वार · ACT I CHAMBER I',    promptHint: 'Step onto the Charnel Plains (Chamber I of V)',    accentColor: '#3ee0a8',  },];export interface HubProximityResult {  station: HubStationSpec | null;  distancePx: number;}/** * Returns the nearest walkable hub station within its interactive halo, or null if none. */export function findNearestHubStation(  playerX: number,  playerY: number): HubProximityResult {  let closest: HubStationSpec | null = null;  let bestDist = Infinity;  for (const st of VAITARANI_HUB_STATIONS) {    const dx = playerX - st.x;    const dy = (playerY - st.y) / 0.8; // Subtle isometric Y weighting    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);    if (dist <= st.interactRadiusPx && dist < bestDist) {      bestDist = dist;      closest = st;    }  }  return {    station: closest,    distancePx: Number.isFinite(bestDist) ? Math.round(bestDist) : Infinity,  };}/** * Returns true if Rudra physically walks into the Northern Charnel Gate trigger zone. */export function isInsideHubFerryGate(playerX: number, playerY: number): boolean {  const dx = playerX - HUB_FERRY_GATE_POS.x;  const dy = playerY - HUB_FERRY_GATE_POS.y;  return Math.hypot(dx, dy) <= 68;}

Ran for 10s
In simple words, here is where your e68bd16 update stands:
You made huge visual and combat improvements, and the code builds cleanly (zero TypeScript errors, all 16 test files pass). However, a few important features are still turned off, and you accidentally cut the game from 8 chambers down to 5.
What You Fixed Well
Clean Title Screen & HUD: The annoying button clutter is gone. The bottom-left bar is neat, the Boss health bar sits cleanly at the top center without overlapping anything, and the Title screen loads with zero errors.
Walkable Hub: Instead of blocking the screen with a big popup menu, the Vaitarani Hub is now a real playable area where you can walk between Savitri (Lotus_Empress), the Homa Shrine, and the Ferry Gate.
Better Bosses & Weapons:
Bosses are much bigger and scarier (2.15× and 2.35× larger).
Enemies stop stealing each other's weapons (your semi-boss uses Emberfang, Ironmaw Gladiators use a proper buckler instead of a boss shield, and Vrikodara uses his skull warlord model and heavy mace/trident).
Materials look like real weathered metal/stone instead of flat plastic.
Less White Flash / Cleaner VFX: You replaced the harsh white hit-flash with a warm golden glow, toned down blinding white explosions, and added dark ink shadows under effects so fighters stay easy to see.
Smart Boss Mechanics: Generic shockwaves no longer destroy Dundubhi's pillars by accident (so you can properly bait him into them), and Vrikodara's Consume inhale + Shards actually work.
What Is Still Broken or Missing
You Cut 8 Chambers Down to 5: You removed Chambers 5, 6, and 7 and made Vrikodara appear in Chamber 5 instead of Chamber 8. Because of that, 4 finished enemy/boss 3D models (Ashen Warlord, Sovereign of the Eternal Wheel, Emberforged Titan, Molten Warlord) and the Raktabija Courtyard map sit completely unused.
Floor Traps & Arena Shapes Are Still Empty: Even though your trap code is written and ready, every single arena currently has zero traps (traps: []) and zero holes/lava gaps (chasmIslands: []). Every room is still basically the same octagon with exit doors sitting near the middle of the floor instead of the walls.
6 Painted Effect Sheets Are Loaded and Ignored: Six art sheets (brazier flames, floor craters, cracks, Vedic impacts, skull/lotus VFX) are loaded in memory and then skipped with void—meaning they never show up on screen.

Action history
As someone who plays Sekiro, Black Myth: Wukong, Ghost of Tsushima, and Hades, your instincts are 100% right. Do not gaslight yourself—the reason the game feels off right now has nothing to do with effort. It is because four specific technical bugs in how your 3D models and VFX are drawn are sabotaging everything on screen.
Here is the exact, honest diagnosis from your code—and why adding more patches on top of this setup has been so frustrating.
1. Why Models Look Stretched Like "Snakes", Blurry, and Unreadable
Why some enemies look like stretched snakes (enemy3dRenderer.ts:430–559):
For Meshy models where the rigged FBX (*_generate.fbx) and textured FBX (*_texture.fbx) don't share exact vertices, your code guesses bone weights at runtime using a 56×56×56 spatial voxel box (transferSkinWeightsViaSpatialGrid). When an arm, cloak, horn, or weapon touches a nearby cell, arm/cape polygons get glued to leg or spine bones. When the skeleton moves, those vertices stretch across the body like spaghetti.
Why you can't make out 3D character details (KaliyugaArena.tsx:1036–1042, 9410–9417):
You render Rudra and enemies into tiny 192×192 pixel offscreen canvases and bosses into 224×224 pixel canvases (with ~40% empty margin so raised axes don't get clipped). That means a boss is only ~130 actual pixels tall—and then the game stretches that blurry stamp up to 320×320 pixels on screen. That is why every boss looks soft and muddy instead of razor-sharp.
2. Why Animations Switch Super Fast and Rudra Feels Choppy
Rudra has zero animation blending (rudra3dRenderer.ts:550–565):
Every time Rudra changes state (idle → run → attack1), renderFrame() runs stopAllAction(); action.reset(); action.play(). Bones hard-teleport in 1 frame with zero crossFadeTo smoothing.
10 FPS Stepped Playback + Snapped Angles:
Rudra and enemies are locked to 10–12 discrete animation frames (stepsPerCycle = 8–10) and 10–12 snapped rotation angles (30°–36° jumps). So instead of smooth 60 FPS Hades / God of War motion, characters snap between poses like a flip-book.
Boss Attack Speed Desync (boss3dRenderer.ts:1418, 1459):
Boss attacks force targetTimeScale = 1.42 * phaseBoost, while frameCache serves quantized 10-step frames—so big heavy swings whip past in ~0.35 seconds with no weight or follow-through.
3. Why You See 2D Image Sprites Popping Into 3D Models Mid-Fight
Look at KaliyugaArena.tsx:9356–9465 (and 9642–9699 / 9902–9942):
Because downloading huge raw FBX + animation files over staticAssetProxy.ts takes 3–15 seconds per file, boss3d.updateAndRender() returns false at the start of the fight. So the game draws an AI-generated 2D sprite sheet (boss2DAsset) for the first several seconds—and then the instant the FBX finishes loading, the boss suddenly morphs into a different-looking 3D model mid-battle.
4. Why VFX Show Square Image Backgrounds (and Why MP4 Videos Won't Fix Slashes)
Why you see rectangular image edges around VFX (vfxFlipbook.ts):
Your VFX sprite sheets are AI-generated PNGs (3×3 / 4×2 grids). When you switched blending from pure additive (lighter) to screen (0.78 alpha) plus an ink under-shadow, any background pixel that isn't pure #000000 (dark red/grey smoke or compression glow) shows up as a visible faint rectangle floating over the arena.
Slicing AI sheets mathematically (sheetWidth / cols) chops off glowing slash arcs that cross grid lines—leaving hard straight box edges on curved slashes.
Should you use .mp4 videos for Demon Slayer attack effects? NO—here is why:
MP4 videos have no transparent background (no alpha channel). You would get the exact same floating rectangle box over your arena, only heavier.
Input lag & browser stutter: Decoding 5–10 overlapping .mp4 clips every time you mash light/heavy attack freezes Chrome and causes 50–150ms seek delay, so slashes won't match your axe swing.
How Hades & Demon Slayer (Hinokami Chronicles) actually create those clean Ufotable-style elemental slashes:
Tapered Ribbon Trails + Ink Outlines (pure math in Canvas/WebGL): Track your axe tip over the last 6–10 frames, draw a smooth curved crescent ribbon with a thick black Sumi-e ink outline on the outside (that is the secret to the Demon Slayer water/flame look!), a glowing crimson/gold gradient core inside, and tapered ember droplets flying off the arc.
Zero rectangular boxes, zero network downloads, razor-sharp at 4K, and 100% locked to the actual weapon swing.
5. The Real Reason 2 Months Felt Like Fighting the Engine
You aren't failing at game design—your architecture is fighting itself. Trying to download unbaked Meshy FBXs in the browser, guess broken bone weights at runtime, snapshot them at 192×192 pixels, fall back to unrelated 2D AI images, and paste 'screen'-blended AI PNG boxes on top will always look stitched together, no matter how many hours you polish it.
If you want that genuine Hades + Demon Slayer feel without throwing away your 2 months of combat tuning, arenas, and bosses, you have two clean ways out (pick ONE and drop the hybrid mess):
Option A — True Live 3D Layer (Best for your 3D models & Sekiro/Wukong weapon weight):
Put ONE real, full-resolution 1440×900 transparent Three.js WebGL canvas directly over your MP4/painted arena floor (matching the isometric camera angle).
Delete the 192×192 sprite baker (hadesSpriteBaker.ts) completely—no more pixelated upscaling, no 10 FPS snapping, full 60 FPS crossFadeTo bone interpolation, and real-time Toon/Cel shader + black ink rim outlines directly on the 3D mesh.
Fix the "snake" models offline once in Blender (import, check bone weights / merge properly, export clean .glb (Draco compressed)—one single 1–3 MB .glb per character with all its animations inside, instead of 15 separate 10 MB .fbx files!).
Option B — True Hades Pre-Rendered 2D Pipeline (Zero runtime 3D bugs):
Render your 3D characters offline in Blender from an isometric camera at 512×512 per frame with smooth 24 FPS animations and toon outlines into transparent WebP/PNG atlases.
Then the browser never loads Three.js, FBX, or bone-weight code at all—everything boots in <1 second at locked 60 FPS, just like Hades.
And for both options:
Delete AI-generated VFX grid PNGs for slashes and replace them with drawn Demon Slayer ink-edged tapered arc ribbons (black Sumi-e outer stroke + blazing fire/lightning inner core) synced directly to Rudra's swing arc. That one change alone removes 90% of the "AI slop" feel immediately.

Here is the exact architectural blueprint to make the live 3D game actually match that art and reach true stylized AAA quality (Demon Slayer: Hinokami Chronicles, Hi-Fi Rush, Guilty Gear Strive, Hades)—without needing UE5 or 8-hour Blender sessions.
Pillar 1: Replace "Plastic 3D" with a True Ufotable / Demon Slayer Shader
Right now, Three.js uses standard PBR (MeshStandardMaterial), which smears smooth grey lighting over Meshy models and makes them look like muddy plastic sitting on top of 2D ink art.
To get the hand-drawn anime / Demon Slayer look in Three.js, we write a custom Cel + Ink Material:
Hard 2-Band / 3-Band Cel Shading (Sharp Shadow Cuts):
Instead of soft grey shading, light cuts across muscles and armor with a razor-sharp anime edge (smoothstep(0.0, 0.04, NdotL)).
Cool Plum/Indigo Shadows + Warm Fire Highlights (The Hades / Ufotable Rule):
Dark areas are never muddy black or grey—they shift toward deep twilight indigo (#181024), while lit faces catch warm saffron/crimson light (#ff9c40). That warm-vs-cool contrast is what makes 3D anime characters pop instantly off dark stone arenas.
Inverted-Hull Black Ink Outlines:
Every 3D character and weapon gets a crisp, dark Sumi-e outline (THREE.BackSide extruded along smoothed normals in #07050a). Suddenly, 3D Rudra and 3D Dundubhi look like they were inked by the exact same artist who drew your arena.
Sharp Anime Rim Light + Impact Frames:
A crisp backlit rim along shoulders, horns, and the Parashu blade edge so silhouettes stay readable even in heavy shadow.
On heavy hits, parries, and posture breaks: 1–2 true anime "Impact Frames" (high-contrast black-ink & blazing-gold flash + brief FOV kick), just like Demon Slayer finishers.
Pillar 2: Real Demon Slayer Ink-Ribbon Slash VFX (Zero PNG Boxes)
This is where 80% of the Demon Slayer identity lives. We throw out the AI-generated PNG slash grids (parashuSlashes3x3) completely and build 3D Blade-Attached Shader Ribbons:
How it works: Attach two empty bone trackers (bladeBase and bladeTip) to Rudra's Parashu and Dundubhi's mace. As the axe swings in 3D space, every frame records a curved spline trail (TriangleStrip mesh) directly behind the moving edge.
What the Custom GLSL Ribbon Shader draws (the Ufotable secret):
Sumi-e Calligraphy Border: A noisy, organic black ink brush edge framing the outside of the elemental arc (using hard alpha discard—no soft translucent box edges!).
Stepped Elemental Core (Agni / Vayu / Vajra):
Agni (Fire / Parashu): Blinding ivory core (#fff3cf) → fierce saffron (#ff8c1a) → blood crimson outer flame (#c91822) with scrolling stylized turbulence ripples.
Vayu (Wind): Razor-sharp pale jade/ash ink streaks with swirling vortex tails.
Vajra (Thunder): Jagged gold-white lightning needles snapping radially off the swing arc.
Brush-Splatter Sparks & Ground Scorch: Sharp diamond/needle sparks and crisp cel-shaded shockwave rings projected flat in 3D ground space (proper isometric perspective, never flat 2D stickers).
Pillar 3: True 60 FPS AAA Animation Feel (Kill the "192px Baker" & "Snakes")
Visually stunning models still feel cheap if the animation snaps or breaks. Three changes fix this permanently:
One Shared Full-Resolution (1440×900) Transparent Three.js Canvas:
Completely delete hadesSpriteBaker.ts (192×192 offscreen stamping, 10-step quantization, 30° yaw snapping) and delete the 2D-to-3D fallback swap (boss2DAsset).

AAA Hitstop & Animation Speed Curves (Why Sekiro / God of War Feel Heavy):
Never play an attack animation at constant 1.0x speed. Use an anticipation-snap-followthrough curve:
Windup (0.65x speed): Rudra pulls the Parashu back so your eye reads the pose.
Strike (2.3x whip): Blade snaps violently through the arc, stretching the Demon Slayer ribbon behind it.
Contact Hitstop (55ms freeze): Simulation and boss freeze for 3 frames while the slash shader & camera kick keep pulsing.
Recovery (0.85x) + .crossFadeTo(0.12s): Smoothly blends back into run/idle with zero bone teleporting.
Clean .glb Pipeline (No More Runtime "Snake" Guessing or 15s Stalls):
Delete transferSkinWeightsViaSpatialGrid (the 56×56×56 runtime voxel guesser causing stretched "snake" meshes).

Synced Corner Brazier Point Lights: Place 4 warm flickering Three.js point lights at the exact coordinates of the 4 glowing braziers in dundubhi_drum_arena, so as Rudra dashes near a corner brazier, warm orange cel-highlights sweep across his armor and axe.
Selective Bloom Post-Processing (Thresholded): Apply bloom strictly to pixel brightness > 0.88 (only the white-hot slash core, weapon embers, and Dundubhi's glowing runes glow—everything else stays razor-sharp ink).
Micro Camera Craft: Smooth exponential camera follow (damp) toward cursor + combat action, subtle camera recoil on hits, and zero screen blur (backdrop-filter banned).
Pillar 5: AAA Visual Polish Checklist (What Makes It Feel Like a Finished Game)
Diegetic Boss Telegraphs: Instead of generic flat red circles, project rotating Sanskrit / Vedic mandala rings onto the 3D floor that snap sharp white-gold the exact frame you should Vajra Parry (Sekiro perilous-attack clarity).

Crucial Meshy / 3D Generation Rules (To Prevent "Snake" Glitches Forever)

1. Why Your Existing 3D Models Look Dark/Muddy—and How We Make Them Bright & Crisp
Right now, two bugs in your renderer are hiding all the detail on your models:
metalness: 1.0 with a weak 0.35 environment (boss3dRenderer.ts): High metalness with almost no environment reflection turns bronze, iron, and ash skin nearly pitch-black against dark floors.
192×192 pixel sprite-baking: Crushes texture resolution and washes out colors before upscaling them back to 320px.
How we make YOUR exact existing 3D models bright, vivid, and anime-readable in Three.js:
Live Full-Resolution Rendering + Albedo Lift Shader: Apply a shader color lift (gamma/saturation boost on the diffuse map) directly on your existing Meshy textures so Rudra's crimson wraps, bronze armor, and Dundubhi's horned hide read bright, rich, and saturated instead of muddy grey.
2-Tone Demon Slayer Cel Shading + Strong Warm Rim Light: Replace dull PBR shadows with sharp anime light cuts—bright warm sunlit/fire highlights, cool deep-plum shadows (never black mud), and a thick glowing amber/gold rim edge separating characters from the dark arena floor.
Crisp Sumi-e Black Ink Outline (BackSide hull): Wraps your existing 3D meshes in clean black line art so they match your hand-inked arenas (dundubhi_drum_arena).
Keep Clean Rigs, Drop Only the Broken Voxel Guesser: Your Ashen_Warborn (Rudra), Dundubhi, Vrikodara, and Lotus_Empress already have clean native skeletons (Path 1 / Path 3)—they don't turn into snakes! Only the Path 2 enemies using transferSkinWeightsViaSpatialGrid break, so we simply stop running that voxel guesser.
2. True Demon Slayer Battle VFX (100% Shader-Driven — Zero New Assets Needed)
Instead of AI PNG sheets that leave rectangular boxes on screen, we attach 3D GLSL Ribbon Trails directly to Rudra's Bloodforged_Trident_Axe and Dundubhi's weapon:
Flame Breathing (Hinokami / Agni Slashes): Sweeping 3D curved ribbon with a jagged Sumi-e black ink outer brush stroke, blazing ivory → saffron-gold → crimson stepped flame bands, and curling ember streaks using hard alpha cutouts (zero boxes).
Thunder / Water Breathing Variants (Stances 1 / 2 / 3):
Smash (Agni): Thick blazing crimson-gold ink waves + ground magma fissures.

Built and verified the Bronze Drum Sanctuary single-chamber 3D prototype using your existing Meshy FBX models from kaalyuga.git upgraded with cel-shading, Sumi-e ink contours, and procedural Demon Slayer elemental slash ribbons.
What Was Built & Upgraded
Your Existing 3D Rigs, Fixed & Brightened:
Rudra (Ashen_Warborn) + Parashu (Bloodforged_Trident_Axe) and Dundubhi (Runebound_Minotaur_Warlord) are loaded locally from public/kaalyuga/ with stabilized in-place Mixamo animation crossfades (AnimationMixer) — no 2D sprite swaps or sudden skeletal snapping.
Replaced dark PBR muddiness with lifted anime texture vibrancy, a 4-step toon/cel gradient shader (MeshToonMaterial), bright rim/fill lighting, white-gold/red cel hit-flashes, and mesh-normalized Sumi-e black ink outlines.
Procedural Demon Slayer Crescent Slash Ribbons:
Added a custom GLSL ink-bordered triangle-strip ribbon system (SlashRibbonController + pooled 3D impact sparks) that renders stepped anime flame/lightning/wind bands synchronized to Rudra's swings:
1 · Agni: Hinokami crimson-orange-white flame arcs (+Damage).
2 · Vajra: Electric blue-gold-white thunder crescent (+Speed & Poise Break).
3 · Vayu: Emerald-silver gale wave (+Wide Cleave Reach).
Chamber Combat & Audio:
Fixed 45° isometric camera with damped follow, trauma shake, and FOV impact kick on crits/parries.
Responsive WASD movement, 3-hit combo (LMB / J), Heavy Cleave (RMB / K), 360° Astra Special (Q), Dash with i-frames (Space), and Perfect Parry / Deflect (Shift / L) that spikes Dundubhi's Poise bar.
Dundubhi Phase 1 & Phase 2 AI with ground telegraphs, destructible Kurukshetra pillars, floating damage numbers, Pause/Victory/Defeat overlays, and layered Web Audio + bgm.mp3 / hit.wav / drum.wav.
Verification Summary

Build & Lint: compile_applet and lint_applet both passed with zero errors.
Headless Browser (1440×900): Verified Title screen (data-ready="true"), ?autostart=1 gameplay entry, WASD movement, Agni/Vajra/Vayu slash ribbons (including catching an initial straight-chord kink + oversized Sumi-e outline bug in Round 1 and re-verifying the smooth curved ink-bordered crescents in Round 2), HUD state/damage sync (Dundubhi 900 → 816, Rudra 100 → 74), KeyP pause/resume, and 0 console errors. Controls
